# Typebars

*Type element feature · code `ELEM:typebar`*

Pivoted typebars (one or two characters each)

**2296 patents**, 1856–1950. Often together with: [Full keyboard, a key per character](../features/sel-keyboard-full.md) (771), [Element struck to the platen](../features/imp-element-to-platen.md) (519), [Adjustment and wear take-up](../features/frm-adjustment-wear.md) (504), [Shift by moving the platen](../features/shift-platen.md) (436), [Pawl-and-rack escapement](../features/esc-pawl-rack.md) (405), [Ink ribbon](../features/ink-ribbon.md) (321), [Visible writing](../features/frm-writing-visible.md) (297), [Roller platen](../features/pap-platen-roller.md) (273)

**Machines:** [Remington Port. 1, 2](../machines/remington-port-1-2.md) (22), [Alexander (1)](../machines/alexander-1.md) (19), [Alexander (2)](../machines/alexander-2.md) (19), [Remington Portable 3](../machines/remington-portable-3.md) (17), [Remington Junior](../machines/remington-junior.md) (14), [Alexander (3)](../machines/alexander-3.md) (14), [Corona 3](../machines/corona-3.md) (14), [Yost 4 - 9](../machines/yost-4-9.md) (11), [Yost 10 - 14](../machines/yost-10-14.md) (11), [Bar-Lock 12](../machines/bar-lock-12.md) (11), [Bar-Lock 14](../machines/bar-lock-14.md) (10), [Smith Premier Simplex](../machines/smith-premier-simplex.md) (10), [Smith Premier Ratiotyp](../machines/smith-premier-ratiotyp.md) (10), [Remington Port. Tab](../machines/remington-port-tab.md) (10), [Caligraph 3](../machines/caligraph-3.md) (9)

| [US15164](../patents/US15164.md) | 1856 | Beach, A. Ely | Pivoted pairs of male and female type-keys meet at a common center to emboss a raised letter on a paper strip, with a clockwork escapement advancing the paper after each stroke. |
| [US65807](../patents/US65807.md) | 1867 | Hall, Thomas | Keyed weighted type-levers strike a common point while a rock-shaft moves a frame by a distance set by the length of a per-key lever, giving proportional spacing. |

| [DE339631](../patents/DE339631.md) | 1921 | Schrey, Rudolf | The light inking roller that the types strike is made of rubber (or a rubber-covered, sanded wooden or hard-rubber roller), so it stays clean and keeps the type face clean for much longer. |
| [DE339789](../patents/DE339789.md) | 1921 | Sandor, Franz | Intermediate levers are carried on two common axes concentric with the printing point, so the effective lever length changes as the key is pressed and the type lever accelerates at impact and on the return swing. |
| [DE339864](../patents/DE339864.md) | 1921 | Abramsohn, Tobias | A plate fixed to the type basket (or to a removable segment) carries stops spaced at the character pitch and a magnet holds the types against the stops, so each type is located by the stops and then soldered in place. |
| [DE343935](../patents/DE343935.md) | 1921 | Himmer, Gustav | Typebars of equal width strike a ribbon over an elastic band that is advanced by the width of the widest type and pulled back by the difference for narrower letters, then the line is transferred to paper. |
| [DE345369](../patents/DE345369.md) | 1921 | Leidenroth, Gustav | Thickened areas on each tie rod are engaged by a comb-like spring-loaded body that locks the keys; a lever and bolt release the trapped type lever and return it to rest. |
| [DE345365](../patents/DE345365.md) | 1921 | Stacke, Bernhard | The type-bar housing is a spherical box folded up on a hinge on a sliding frame, with a raised platform carrying punctuation and space keys and a cut-out so the writing is freely visible. |
| [DE345902](../patents/DE345902.md) | 1921 |  | Each pivoted type bar carries ten digit types and is raised to the correct height by the lever mechanism; one hammer strikes all bars, and zeros are printed by blow bars when needed. |
| [DE346703](../patents/DE346703.md) | 1922 | Burboa, Juan Gualberto Holguin | Selector disc valves between a few keys and the typebars route suction from foot-driven bellows to pneumatic bellows that move the typebars. |
| [DE346814](../patents/DE346814.md) | 1922 | Scheffer, Rindert | Each type lever has a slot engaged by a pin on the key lever and a common pin, so the intermediate piece is dropped; a spring returns the lever and each lever can be pulled out individually. |
| [DE347525](../patents/DE347525.md) | 1922 | Raap, Abraham Van | The inventor's arrangement lets the superimposed characters print in correct position without lifting the paper roller or the whole carriage; the excerpt states the problem and the full mechanism is not in the text read. |
| [DE348158](../patents/DE348158.md) | 1922 |  | Type levers mounted in a fixed frame are held in rest by a liftable abutment moved by a hand crank; lowering the type levers out of use folds the machine into a small space with simple means. |
| [DE348778](../patents/DE348778.md) | 1922 |  | The bearing body is made of two sheet-metal discs or a hollowed piece spaced apart at their edges, so the slotted edges have reduced friction surfaces and the guide slots hold the type levers accurately. |
| [DE349015](../patents/DE349015.md) | 1922 | Schueler, Franz | Put a roller on the guide arm so that it runs onto the guide plate surface just before the type reaches the platen, so almost no sliding wear occurs. |
| [DE350262](../patents/DE350262.md) | 1922 |  | A narrow arched rail buffer stop is fixed at both ends over thin sound-absorbing spacers on the bearing segment, so it does not touch the bearing directly; it is easily replaced and adjusted. |
| [DE351534](../patents/DE351534.md) | 1922 |  | Each swing-frame journal is mounted independently and can be slightly adjusted in each direction, so the universal rail is moved freely and evenly by all type levers. |
| [DE351950](../patents/DE351950.md) | 1922 | Sandor, Franz | A pull rod on the front of the key housing releases the universal-rail lever so a type lever can be removed without holding the release. |
| [DE352314](../patents/DE352314.md) | 1922 |  | A crank on the operating axis is set by a firing rod from the keyboard into four rotational positions (normal addition, total or subtotal, subtraction, print without addition), each selecting a ribbon zone and whether the minus sign is carried to the hammer-driven type bar. |
| [DE352316](../patents/DE352316.md) | 1922 |  | Two presettable stops are linked so that when one is active the other is switched off; the stroke of the type-bar lever is limited by whichever stop is in the working position, so the bar prints the keyed number or the counter value. |
| [DE354576](../patents/DE354576.md) | 1922 |  | A second, secondary type carrier on the same segment is struck by the same key, so two different characters print on one position with one keystroke. |
| [DE357960](../patents/DE357960.md) | 1922 |  | Fixed type letters are held in place by supports at impact, and levers drop flaps onto the rods so the selected character is positioned for printing. |
| [DE358203](../patents/DE358203.md) | 1922 |  | Intermediate lever pivots can be removed from the segment, and a parallelogram linkage arrangement lets each type lever be taken out or fitted individually. |
| [DE358204](../patents/DE358204.md) | 1922 | Mayer, Otto | A flexible sheathed pull cable connects each key to its type lever, with a return spring, so the motion path is independent of the cable direction. |
| [DE358545](../patents/DE358545.md) | 1922 |  | Decimal and column tabulator keys preset the calculating machine for addition or subtraction per column automatically, with ribbon colour tied to the mode. |
| [DE359445](../patents/DE359445.md) | 1922 | Immervoll, Albert | An adjustable guide piece on the type-lever segment engages a groove in the type lever end, and it can be raised or lowered to adjust it. |
| [DE359609](../patents/DE359609.md) | 1922 | Geb, Lehmann | The support bracket is hung on arms and springs from the type basket plate, so it gives way when the type lever falls back. |
| [DE359978](../patents/DE359978.md) | 1922 | Melcher, Eduard | A roll-up drive band carries removable pin-like projections that push the extended key levers in sequence, so one band blank can be pinned for any text. |
| [DE359979](../patents/DE359979.md) | 1922 | Gagliazzo, Carlo | A connecting lever and pivot placed on slanted lines raise the speed of the type lever for a stronger impact. |
| [DE359986](../patents/DE359986.md) | 1922 |  | The type pieces are attached in a jig: the carrier frame is clamped adjustably on the machine frame, and each stop is set against a fixed reference mark visible in a window so all stops reach one defined position before soldering. |
| [DE360379](../patents/DE360379.md) | 1922 |  | The guide piece is made rigid, with the centres of its two arcs on a line that crosses the arcs before their start, so levers are guided past the closest point; the curved walls are cut with a crown milling cutter from both sides and smoothed by a wedge-shaped anvil. |
| [DE364445](../patents/DE364445.md) | 1922 |  | Intermediate levers are movably mounted between the key linkage and the type-lever linkage, their lower ends joined by a rod to downward levers, so a key stroke can be transferred to the type lever independently of the carriage movement and the type lever can be released before it reaches the platen. |
| [DE365591](../patents/DE365591.md) | 1922 |  | A low web guides the typebar through a recess on its underside, below the types. |
| [DE366615](../patents/DE366615.md) | 1923 |  | A toggle rod links each intermediate lever to its type lever; the toggle rods swing in planes parallel to the median plane, so motion transfers under more favourable angles, and the type lever segment can be raised or lowered for case switching. |
| [DE366616](../patents/DE366616.md) | 1923 | Offenhauer, Arnold | Hook-shaped tie rods, all of equal length, have pinned ends in slots of the type levers and run in a circular-arc comb with slots concentric to the type-lever axis, giving non-clamping motion with increasing type-lever speed and interchangeable levers. |
| [DE366623](../patents/DE366623.md) | 1923 | Schneider, Hermann | Keys on a lazy-tongs (Nuremberg scissors) linkage drive types through springy wires in flexible tubes, and the platen and carriage fold in half on hinges. |
| [DE366719](../patents/DE366719.md) | 1923 |  | Key levers are cranked sideways in their middle group to clear space for the type-lever basket, and bent-arm key levers drive the type levers through intermediate levers of different lengths, which also accelerate the strike, lowering the machine height. |
| [DE366720](../patents/DE366720.md) | 1923 | Immervoll, Albert | The segment is carried on vertical guide rails between two pairs of springs that hold it centred; a curved guide piece on a shaft, driven by a lever, lifts it to the other end position and it is held there, so shifting is smooth and fixed. |
| [DE366846](../patents/DE366846.md) | 1923 | Krause, Julius | Each key prints either a single letter or a three-letter syllable from the same key, and the carriage-feed gear is coupled alternately to a gear that steps one letter or three letters. |
| [DE367769](../patents/DE367769.md) | 1923 | Lehmann, Karl August | The guide tabs on each type head are made of parts parallel to the lever shafts so the play is limited, with less play for the outermost type levers than for the middle one, giving more even spacing and straighter lines. |
| [DE368219](../patents/DE368219.md) | 1923 | Reisser, Adolf | Two spring-loaded guide arms swing on vertical axles and are pushed by the striking nose of the type lever; they form a toggle-like mechanism that centres every type lever and reduces friction. |
| [DE369303](../patents/DE369303.md) | 1923 | Scheffer, Rindert | A single bearing bracket has two offset rows of radial incisions, one row holding the key levers and the other the type levers, with a pin on each key lever engaging the type lever; the bracket is made by moulding so no machining is needed. |
| [DE369304](../patents/DE369304.md) | 1923 | Graff, Maximilian | A rocker under the key lever extensions completes the shift travel to a stop when any key is struck, through a slide and angle lever on the shift rod. |
| [DE369305](../patents/DE369305.md) | 1923 |  | The shift key only releases a power-driven linkage that lifts the carriage and latches it until the next type impression. |
| [DE369308](../patents/DE369308.md) | 1923 | Reisser, Adolf | The guide arms get nose-like overlapping extensions that touch the opposite arm when swung apart, so each arm's nose acts as its own stroke limiter. |
| [DE370303](../patents/DE370303.md) | 1923 | Tippmann, Kurt Fritz | A typewriter is set up for multi-line accounting slips where the first column is pre-printed or set by special keys, and several typebar sets strike together so that each booking entry is written once into the grid. |
| [DE371608](../patents/DE371608.md) | 1923 | Buehler, Franz | Key levers drive type levers through sets of interchangeable intermediate levers with cams at different distances from the fulcrum; swapping the lever sets changes which key drives which type lever, so the cipher can be varied and then reset for ordinary writing. |
| [DE371984](../patents/DE371984.md) | 1923 |  | The type is clamped adjustably on a sliding frame and sighted against a glass plate marked with type-width and type-height lines on micrometer hinges, the plate being swung out before the hot attachment; the type is then fixed to the lever. |
| [DE373914](../patents/DE373914.md) | 1923 |  | The set of type levers is raised on a detent in the working position and folded down in the transport position, with the key-to-type linkage disengaged by a toothed segment and the spacer key moved inward below the keyboard; a movable frame part rises and lowers the type-lever frame. |
| [DE374369](../patents/DE374369.md) | 1923 |  | Pawls on swinging rails follow perforations in the punched tape and drop into recesses of a rotating roller, so each rail shifts sideways; the rails act directly on the key levers through adjustable screws, and a common pressure plate releases all pawls together. |
| [DE374370](../patents/DE374370.md) | 1923 |  | Split the shift rail into a base body moving horizontally and a rail that rises and falls on it with the bearing segment, keeping a constant relationship to the typebars. |
| [DE374424](../patents/DE374424.md) | 1923 |  | A resilient arm, cut free from the type-lever shaft by a slot, takes up the excess force of the stroke at the bounce stop, so the lever stops more softly while the spring-back is still driven by the stored energy. |
| [DE374969](../patents/DE374969.md) | 1923 |  | Rails controlled by the punched tape act directly on the key levers, via adjustable screws, with the pawls lifted free of the tape after use. |
| [DE375720](../patents/DE375720.md) | 1923 | Berneburg, Eduard | Each typebar head has a spur-like projection that engages under a bar on the frame just before the stop, so no guide head is needed. |
| [DE375721](../patents/DE375721.md) | 1923 |  | The point where motion is transferred is moved into the same plane of oscillation as the intermediate and key levers by a lateral bend on the key lever or intermediate lever, and the bearing wire seats on a sloped wall so no rattle occurs. |
| [DE376272](../patents/DE376272.md) | 1923 |  | The type lever segment is a triangular or three-legged frame of thin punched sheet metal tied into the main frame, carrying the key lever bearing rail and the connecting part bearings, which keeps the structure stiff and light. |
| [DE376478](../patents/DE376478.md) | 1923 | Rautmann, Kurt | The type face is a ring with a small central point made by an adjustable screw bolt set into the lever head, so the point depth can be set to suit the paper or matrix. |
| [DE376561](../patents/DE376561.md) | 1923 |  | The support arm of the intermediate member pivots on the pawl carrier about an axis across the carrier's rotation, so the link can move freely and can also be switched off. |
| [DE376560](../patents/DE376560.md) | 1923 | Pereszlenyi, Ludwig | The keyboard, key levers and type levers sit in a flat box housing, with the paper carriage running in a slotted tube that slides over the box, so both fold together for transport. Type levers are mounted on a comb-like arc segment and each carries upper and lower case types. |
| [DE376558](../patents/DE376558.md) | 1923 | Haller, Eugen | Each key moves an auxiliary lever with one of three different deflections, advancing the carriage ratchet by one, two or three teeth. |
| [DE376559](../patents/DE376559.md) | 1923 |  | A zone plate with hooks at different distances, mounted on the ribbon-fork lever not the universal rail, selects the ribbon stroke or none for stencils. |
| [DE376721](../patents/DE376721.md) | 1923 |  | Key levers are arranged to converge toward the typebar pivots, and each key lever is carried with its type lever on a thin-walled bracket. The brackets hang from a removable segment, and the space bar is hinged back so it clears the packing space. |
| [DE377034](../patents/DE377034.md) | 1923 | Herlt, Franz | A switchable segment carrier, moved by shift-key levers, brings the upper or lower case types into the printing position. Its upper part is adjustable toward the type stop to reset the strike point against the platen. |
| [DE377917](../patents/DE377917.md) | 1923 | Grosser, G F | The universal rail (switch bracket) with its drive parts is fixed to the carriage guide beam, which can be lifted as one unit with the carriage, spring housing and return gear, so the parts can be assembled and adjusted outside the machine. |
| [DE377918](../patents/DE377918.md) | 1923 |  | Very short type levers are pivoted in an arc on a curved axis, and graded intermediate levers connect each type lever to its key connecting rod. The keys then strike the platen with equal strokes in a narrow basket. |
| [DE380642](../patents/DE380642.md) | 1923 |  | Widen the slots at the exit point of the arc axis so a burr cannot foul the type lever, and allow smoothing or softer metals such as brass. |
| [DE381278](../patents/DE381278.md) | 1923 |  | The latching pawls are released automatically by pressing the held shift key down a little further. |
| [DE381357](../patents/DE381357.md) | 1923 | Burg, Hubert | A button lever drives an intermediate lever, which lifts the type lever and then a handlebar knocks it down; a comb holds the levers and a slot lets a lever be removed. |
| [DE381361](../patents/DE381361.md) | 1923 | Hundt, Otto | A cleaning brush hangs vertically from an adjustable bracket, so fine flexible bristles brush the typebar heads as they swing through and shake dirt off. |
| [DE381432](../patents/DE381432.md) | 1923 |  | Single-arm key levers act through a two-arm intermediate lever pivoting on the common axis, cutting key travel to 15-17 mm. |
| [DE381441](../patents/DE381441.md) | 1923 | Burg, Hubert | Place the carriage feed and ribbon movement drive parts between or just above the key levers, driven by a divided sector lever on a shaft. |
| [DE381664](../patents/DE381664.md) | 1923 | Sandor, Franz | Intermediate lever fulcrums rest freely on a console so the bearing tolerance can be looser, while keeping the variable lever length of the parent patent. |
| [DE381818](../patents/DE381818.md) | 1923 | Arpad, Krejniker | A segment has slots on both outside and inside, so half of each type lever runs on the outside and the other half on the inside, making it more compact. |
| [DE383031](../patents/DE383031.md) | 1923 | Geb, Lehmann | A separate, stronger rail runs under all key levers and is moved by the release button, pulling the knocked-together type levers apart without touching the universal rail. |
| [DE383034](../patents/DE383034.md) | 1923 | Burg, Hubert | The rest abutment is a sector disk with notches that seat the type lever heads between its elevations, so a bent lever no longer fits its bed and is noticed; it may be fixed to the frame or inserted only for cleaning. |
| [DE384111](../patents/DE384111.md) | 1923 | Burg, Hubert | A triangular intermediate lever pushes a handlebar with a projection that lifts the type lever at its pin, using a slot or an intermediate link; the handlebar then takes the lever over. |
| [DE385379](../patents/DE385379.md) | 1923 | Sandor, Franz | A segment behind the type levers, moved by the universal rail, pivots an angle lever and sleeve that a detent plate sets; the position is shown in a front window and also controls ribbon switching and a secret lock. |
| [DE385563](../patents/DE385563.md) | 1923 |  | An electromagnet built into the machine, closed by a contact common to all key levers, energizes the selected key lever part way down, completes the stroke and prints, then de-energizes before the lever reaches the bottom. |
| [DE385564](../patents/DE385564.md) | 1923 |  | The intermediate frame is a horizontal lower rail with a type lever bearing segment on posts, which is easily exchangeable, so the type levers and linkage can be assembled outside the machine and then connected to the button levers. |
| [DE385567](../patents/DE385567.md) | 1923 |  | Press part of the ribbon fork resiliently against the platen by a spring, without hindering the ribbon's sideways movement. |
| [DE385648](../patents/DE385648.md) | 1923 | Herlt, Franz | Each intermediate lever bearing is a U-shaped body cut and bent from sheet metal, so the bearings can be placed at offset axes on a crosspiece of a switchable segment carrier. |
| [DE385865](../patents/DE385865.md) | 1923 |  | The type is clamped against a support matrix and the lever head, and a mechanical lifting device pushes the type or die along the lever so it sits at the correct font height from the pivot point. |
| [DE386399](../patents/DE386399.md) | 1923 |  | Each button lever turns a toothed segment that engages a rack on the rear of the stamped type bar; cover plates riding over each other keep the segment in line with the rack and relieve tooth loading. |
| [DE386400](../patents/DE386400.md) | 1923 | Mueller, Albert | The shift key returns immediately when released, and a locking lever holds the platen frame shifted until the next type key trips a gravity-return frame. |
| [DE386408](../patents/DE386408.md) | 1923 | Burg, Hubert | The rest bearing is rigidly fixed to two side arms pivoted on the frame, with a pair of links and a support housing, so the type levers swing down to rest and the machine folds into its housing for transport. |
| [DE386556](../patents/DE386556.md) | 1923 |  | A displaceable rod with extensions acts on angle rails and hammers, and a parallel-sliding rail with toothing holds the set levers in their set position while the other parts return. |
| [DE388931](../patents/DE388931.md) | 1924 | Kleefeld, Ferdinand M | The key lever runs straight down behind a common straight bearing body, with an angle lever whose longer arm drives the type lever through a handlebar and whose shorter arm links to the button lever, giving a large leverage in a compact layout. |
| [DE389565](../patents/DE389565.md) | 1924 |  | Fix two bodies to each typebar: one with no characters to run in the guide, and one carrying the characters above it. |
| [DE389950](../patents/DE389950.md) | 1924 | C, August Hegeling | An angle lever between key lever and type lever, with pivots placed so the weights of the linkage balance, returns the levers to rest without a separate spring. |
| [DE389952](../patents/DE389952.md) | 1924 | C, August Hegeling | A locking slide on one guide segment has edge-open slots, so push rods can be dropped in radially from outside and held by the closure slide. |
| [DE389954](../patents/DE389954.md) | 1924 | C, August Hegeling | A type head guide frame moves toward the platen on each key stroke and the type head sits in its frame, so it is guided and damped during impact. |
| [DE390129](../patents/DE390129.md) | 1924 |  | A wide felt or cushion pad sits under the straight and cranked parts of each type lever with a rounded edge near the pivot so the lever rests on it on return, and the frame and covers are sound-absorbing and form a closed space around the working parts. |
| [DE389953](../patents/DE389953.md) | 1924 | C, August Hegeling | A two-armed ribbon fork with an open top slot is hung on the swinging type head guide near its axis, so the ribbon drops in from above with free edges and passes the strike point. |
| [DE391504](../patents/DE391504.md) | 1924 | Revesz, Stefan | A high-speed typewriter with several keyboards that print letter groups simultaneously, with the carriage stepping by the group's actual width. |
| [DE392071](../patents/DE392071.md) | 1924 | Ainardi, Guiseppe | A three-legged support holds the type levers in writing position and can be folded down by a sliding member, lowering the height of the machine. |
| [DE393932](../patents/DE393932.md) | 1924 | C, August Hegeling | The segment carrier is raised and lowered on balls in side guides, with wedge-shaped stops setting its stroke, so the guides are in place when inserted. |
| [DE395643](../patents/DE395643.md) | 1924 | Kunze, Richard | Each bearing axle is as thick as its spacing, with a central annular groove engaged by a guide web, so no screws or stepped grooves are needed. |
| [DE395585](../patents/DE395585.md) | 1924 | Reisser, Adolf | An intermediate lever with eccentric tooth segment is shaped to nest under a cranked type lever, reducing overall height. |
| [DE395583](../patents/DE395583.md) | 1924 | Michelsen, Carl | A stationary print unit with a carriage moving in line direction and a writing plate sliding crosswise, holding the base posting sheet and underlying sheets flat in any position. |
| [DE397321](../patents/DE397321.md) | 1924 | Farina, Domenico | A rotatable universal rail with lugs of different lengths changes how far the key levers swing it, hence how many steps the carriage advances. |
| [DE398643](../patents/DE398643.md) | 1924 | Kriebel, Walter | A thumb-driven slide, latched by a pawl, sets a stop that rotates the type head to the upper case; the latch releases when the type lever falls so the shift returns on its own. |
| [DE392075](../patents/DE392075.md) | 1924 |  | Put the extra dead or umlaut keys at the side, outside the unchanged main keyboard, in room freed by moving shift and control keys, so the combs and lever bearings are the same for all variants. |
| [DE400384](../patents/DE400384.md) | 1924 |  | An optical microscope mounted on the same slide as the type-piece clamp, with height adjustment, is used to aim the type precisely before soldering the lever. |
| [DE400602](../patents/DE400602.md) | 1924 | F, Naehmaschinen Und Fahrraeder | The key levers roll on a bearing surface instead of pivoting, and the intermediate-lever fulcrum is placed so the drive arm change is compensated, giving even acceleration to the stop. |
| [DE400605](../patents/DE400605.md) | 1924 | Tilgert, Hildegard | Stack two typewriters so each key of the upper three-character machine drives a key lever of the lower single-character machine, whose lever frame slides sideways when the shift key is pressed. |
| [DE393931](../patents/DE393931.md) | 1924 |  | A rod moving through the machine resets each adjuster one by one, and holders are locked against movement, with a latch securing the general erase. |
| [DE402629](../patents/DE402629.md) | 1924 | Lang, Heinrich | Typebars made as punch tools, with counter levers striking from below through a slotted plate, punch characters like typing. |
| [DE403512](../patents/DE403512.md) | 1924 | Knotz, Franz | A key only releases a spring-loaded drop shoe that a cam on a constantly turning shaft then catches and carries to throw the typebar. |
| [DE403517](../patents/DE403517.md) | 1924 |  | Support the push frame on adjustable conical pivot pins, one eccentric and lockable, so its working edge can be set parallel without bending. |
| [DE403510](../patents/DE403510.md) | 1924 |  | Mount the guide head on a swinging pivot so it rests away from the roller and moves up to it just as the type strikes, letting the ribbon stay in front. |
| [DE403513](../patents/DE403513.md) | 1924 | Knotz, Franz | An arm on the rear of each key lever reaches the intermediate lever and strikes it directly, so the type lever can be driven by hand without motor power. |
| [DE403931](../patents/DE403931.md) | 1924 |  | The anvil is a hollow tube of two springy wound wires embedded in the segment groove, which damps impact noise without loss of print sharpness. |
| [DE404176](../patents/DE404176.md) | 1924 |  | A special key bypasses the common movement member so only the ribbon drive and the accent typebar act, with no letter-spacing step. |
| [DE405676](../patents/DE405676.md) | 1924 | Lock, Andreas | A double-armed lever struck near the print point drives a second double lever that pushes the pawl carrier back to release the ratchet wheel, which shortens the path and reduces rattle. |
| [DE389243](../patents/DE389243.md) | 1924 | Motoren, Daimler | Beveled facing edges on the two ratchet halves and a beveled knife tip let the knife slide from one half to the other, nearly silently. |
| [DE406089](../patents/DE406089.md) | 1924 |  | Fixed adjustable stops on a guide post for highest and lowest positions, plus an extra movable stop for the middle position operated by its own shift lever. |
| [DE406095](../patents/DE406095.md) | 1924 | Motoren, Daimler | Ball bearing carried by the two fork ends of the type lever, with the inner race formed by threaded cones so the bearing fits within the lever shaft and stays flush with the fork ends. |
| [DE406088](../patents/DE406088.md) | 1924 |  | A friction bar on the typebar linkage is pressed by a roller against a continuously rotating friction shaft, then released just before impact so inertia completes the stroke. |
| [DE406254](../patents/DE406254.md) | 1924 | Maschf, Kappel | A roller angle lever lets the escapement anchor disconnect from the universal rail at high stroke speed so the carriage steps almost immediately after printing; the same link is lifted by accent keys to stop spacing. |
| [DE406623](../patents/DE406623.md) | 1924 |  | A central spring acts against all type lever movement, and each lever has a stationary individual adjustment so strike force is uniform. |
| [DE407417](../patents/DE407417.md) | 1924 |  | A disengageable motor drive transfers loose type bars to a printing position after line setting, then returns them to rest after printing. |
| [DE407756](../patents/DE407756.md) | 1924 |  | The segment is grooved or cut out at the type lever bearing point, leaving right-angled webs carrying the driven axle, which reduces guide surfaces and stops lateral fluttering. |
| [DE408423](../patents/DE408423.md) | 1925 |  | A plate-shaped ratchet wheel with angular teeth on a slightly inclined shaft lets a vertical escapement fit in the small space. |
| [DE408431](../patents/DE408431.md) | 1925 |  | Two independently adjustable guide jaw carriers are inclined by conical screws in opposite slots, to set both width and sideways position. |
| [DE408953](../patents/DE408953.md) | 1925 |  | Typebars hinge on a fixed support and fold down while the intermediate levers' pivots shift together on a toggle-moved support. |
| [DE409629](../patents/DE409629.md) | 1925 |  | An arm on the carriage-shift frame rests on an adjustable stop on the machine base so the position holds. |
| [DE409631](../patents/DE409631.md) | 1925 |  | Flexible springs wound on a bolt in the tie rod engage a ring-shaped impact rail at the end of travel, so the spring is loaded through the drive link. |
| [DE409996](../patents/DE409996.md) | 1925 |  | Two switching bridges let the width setting travel less for wide characters, making key effort even; suspended in stable or unstable equilibrium to cut work. |
| [DE411804](../patents/DE411804.md) | 1925 |  | The swing weight is placed in a toggle linkage that is detached from the key drive, so the weight stretches the knee lever at the end of the type lever stroke for a slow but forceful type stop. |
| [DE411806](../patents/DE411806.md) | 1925 |  | A fixed stop or anvil stops the type lever just before it touches the platen, and a movable retarder opposite it slows the lever before it reaches the anvil, timed for a quiet stop. |
| [DE389080](../patents/DE389080.md) | 1925 | Kraudzun, Franz | Arrange all typebar bearings and guides in segment form so that all levers except the key levers are identical and can be punched from one die, in a riveted sheet-metal frame. |
| [DE411807](../patents/DE411807.md) | 1925 |  | The platen is replaced by a measuring device: each type lever strikes a bolt, the impact deflects a writing lever that records the impact energy as a curve on a paper strip, so type levers can be set mechanically. |
| [DE412034](../patents/DE412034.md) | 1925 | Castellazzi, Alfred | The type lever swings in the same plane as the key lever, driven by a partial toothing that gives only the start of the stroke, then flies free and is returned by a nose; both levers sit on one small base plate that can be removed as a unit. |
| [DE412930](../patents/DE412930.md) | 1925 |  | The guide slots for the accent levers are not radial to the segment centre but cut nearer the segment circumference, and the accent levers are bent outside their guide to clear the other levers. |
| [DE414331](../patents/DE414331.md) | 1925 |  | The guide comb for the key levers also serves as the bearing for the intermediate lever, with its upper edge rolled into a sleeve, and the type-lever rest cushion sits just above that bearing, saving space. |
| [DE415691](../patents/DE415691.md) | 1925 | Pfahl, Winterling & | A sequence-dependent interlock between latch bolts, a locking comb that holds the intermediate levers, and the protective cover. |
| [DE416148](../patents/DE416148.md) | 1925 | Lock, Andreas | Each key lever drive arm is made in two parts, with a leg that clamps onto the stop arm by a slot, pin and spring locking pin, so the leg can be exchanged without lifting the type lever. |
| [DE416149](../patents/DE416149.md) | 1925 |  | Each key lever is rigidly connected to its type lever by a handlebar, so the type lever is driven all the way to the paper roller without loose play, with the universal rail acting only during the momentum phase. |
| [DE417061](../patents/DE417061.md) | 1925 |  | The attachment points of the handlebars on the side type levers are shifted rearward so all type levers reach the same distance from the platen. |
| [DE417062](../patents/DE417062.md) | 1925 |  | A wire recoil bracket behind the type-lever segment has looped, more flexible ends so the outermost levers meet a strongly resilient stop and snap back. |
| [DE417063](../patents/DE417063.md) | 1925 |  | The type-lever carrier slides in a guide opposite the machine frame, so the frame and platen stay fixed while the type levers are brought to the printing position. |
| [DE417379](../patents/DE417379.md) | 1925 |  | A single crank that raises or lowers the typebar segment is coupled to the shift lock release, carriage shift frame latch and centre carriage lock, so folding the machine forces the correct transport state. |
| [DE417732](../patents/DE417732.md) | 1925 | Rotter, Arthur | A tapered ink container pivoted on a type-lever arm has outlet openings covered by absorbent fabric; the striking type presses the container and inks the face, and centrifugal force keeps the ink moving toward the outlets. |
| [DE417734](../patents/DE417734.md) | 1925 |  | Felt strips on the support sit on a sponge-rubber insert, which absorbs impact and prevents springback and permanent dents. |
| [DE420860](../patents/DE420860.md) | 1925 |  | The inclined engagement surface is twisted along its length so each intermediate lever, despite different stroke, moves the universal rail by the same amount. |
| [DE420858](../patents/DE420858.md) | 1925 |  | A rotatable shaft carried by the shift frame lets dead accent keys drive the ribbon lifter so the accent takes the colour zone of the adjacent letter without advancing the carriage. |
| [DE420856](../patents/DE420856.md) | 1925 |  | The intermediate lever between the key and the typebar is an angle lever that overlaps the front comb in rest, shortening the whole. |
| [DE422362](../patents/DE422362.md) | 1925 | Michelsen, Carl | A push-only linkage with two pivots: rear-arm push rod lifts an intermediate lever that swings the typebar. |
| [DE422905](../patents/DE422905.md) | 1925 |  | Typebars rest floating on a steel wire and band guide, and the key or intermediate lever is made spring-flexible so the impact is softened. |
| [DE423387](../patents/DE423387.md) | 1926 | Roscher, Erich | A ring ribbon with several colour zones is moved on a double-ring frame, with a lever lifting it to bring a chosen zone to the print point and ink pads fed from reservoirs re-inking each zone. |
| [DE424871](../patents/DE424871.md) | 1926 |  | A pivoted link with two teeth of finer pitch than the camshaft always engages at the same relative position, giving a fixed run-up of about 1 mm. |
| [DE427091](../patents/DE427091.md) | 1926 |  | The segment carrier runs in four ball bearings, two fixed to the rear wall and two on front abutments adjustable by bending, so it can be swivelled for exact transverse alignment. |
| [DE427092](../patents/DE427092.md) | 1926 |  | The driving finger on the swinging switch bracket is pivoted so a slider moved by the accent type levers turns it aside, so it no longer acts on the letter shift; alternatively the bracket is raised out of reach. |
| [DE427808](../patents/DE427808.md) | 1926 | F, Naehmaschinen Und Fahrraeder | The cam slot in each type lever is shaped so the arm acted on by the intermediate lever shortens during the stroke, giving a slight lift and constant acceleration. |
| [DE428298](../patents/DE428298.md) | 1926 |  | Redraw the letters so all have the same width and similar ink area: block letters, with narrow letters given wide serifs or overstrokes and m, w and M shortened in the middle. |
| [DE428720](../patents/DE428720.md) | 1926 | Anavi, Nissim | Two pressed half-disc plates with type lever slots are held apart by spacer and intermediate plates, and an intermediate plate with a circumferential groove holds the axle, which is clamped by nuts. |
| [DE429365](../patents/DE429365.md) | 1926 | Emme, Heinrich | The guide carrier is above the typebars, in one piece or bolted to the guide, so drive and bar shape are free. |
| [DE429381](../patents/DE429381.md) | 1926 |  | The coupling pawl is spring-mounted in a longitudinal slot so the key can still reach its end position. |
| [DE431518](../patents/DE431518.md) | 1926 |  | A swinging shift frame held locked in the middle position, freed by either shift key, with a spring returning it to centre. |
| [DE432120](../patents/DE432120.md) | 1926 |  | A camshaft drives tension members that strike the type levers; stripping members set how long each tension member engages, adjusted individually by pivoting adjusting elements with hole rows, or together through a lever-driven transmission for fine adjustment. |
| [DE432260](../patents/DE432260.md) | 1926 | Vondra, Karl | Each type lever carries an auxiliary lever so any two key levers pressed in either order print one character. |
| [DE432121](../patents/DE432121.md) | 1926 |  | Each type lever gets its own adjusting member with a resilient disc and eccentric on an axle, so each one can be set on its own; the arms and axles form a rigid whole. |
| [DE434836](../patents/DE434836.md) | 1926 |  | An overload clutch on the camshaft consists of a socket with ratchet teeth on one end face, pushed against a second ratchet socket by a spring; the sockets slip when the type lever stops early, so the drive links do not break. |
| [DE434837](../patents/DE434837.md) | 1926 | Thorsch, Siegfried | One common movable stop bar, positioned by one shift key, limits the swing of all typebars so that either type of the two on a bar prints. |
| [DE434845](../patents/DE434845.md) | 1926 |  | The universal rail frame acts on a bell crank connected directly to the ribbon fork, and a manual slide sets colour zone and case through pins. |
| [DE434848](../patents/DE434848.md) | 1926 |  | The buffer ring is set at an acute angle to the plane of the type lever bearing, and the type lever has matching inclined noses, so the stop force moves the cross-section away from the eye and presses the lever down against its bearing. |
| [DE435157](../patents/DE435157.md) | 1926 |  | The drive rail is suddenly stopped and retracted before the type reaches the platen, so the type lever finishes its stroke on its own momentum while the rail is ready for the next lever. |
| [DE435853](../patents/DE435853.md) | 1926 | Puttemans, Hubert Auguste | A frame that swings on axles under the keyboard is pulled by a spring and driven by one or two electromagnets placed at the back of the machine, with pawls on a comb that engage the key levers, so the key motion is transmitted directly. |
| [DE435854](../patents/DE435854.md) | 1926 |  | Switch modes by shifting one of the two drive-lever mechanisms (key levers with their bearing block, or the power lever carriage) so the key lever either engages the type lever directly or the power linkage. |
| [DE435860](../patents/DE435860.md) | 1926 |  | A wide-slotted resilient tongue on each type lever strikes a fixed curved stop just before impact, giving a long springy rebound; side levers are bent opposite ways to cancel twisting. |
| [DE436297](../patents/DE436297.md) | 1926 | Buchner, Franz | Specially shaped interchangeable crimping jaw has a slot for the guide bit while the neck is gripped by pressure pieces, with a stop for precise cranking. |
| [DE436500](../patents/DE436500.md) | 1926 |  | A second spur at mid-height of the type, just below the lowest character, engages a slotted guide with lateral guide surfaces, so the type is guided without shading the print point. |
| [DE436724](../patents/DE436724.md) | 1926 |  | Guide the double-armed key lever in slots in both legs of a U-shaped stamped bearing comb and link its rear arm to the intermediate lever by a freely movable wire link. |
| [DE438188](../patents/DE438188.md) | 1926 | Knotz, Franz | A swinging shoe on a rocker arm is released by a key-lever pawl, taken by a cam for one stroke and then re-latched even while the key is still held. |
| [DE440060](../patents/DE440060.md) | 1927 |  | A coupling link normally acts as a single stop and is held into the drive member only when the permanent-stop device is set, so faults fail safely. |
| [DE440775](../patents/DE440775.md) | 1927 | Geb, Merz | Flat connecting rods and intermediate levers with an upward bend let the keyboard sit lower while the type-lever segment stays vertical. |
| [DE441007](../patents/DE441007.md) | 1927 |  | The guide heads are embossed in the die together with the character, so they and their distance from the letter form in one stamping with no further rework. |
| [DE442268](../patents/DE442268.md) | 1927 |  | A lifting shaft with a release cam, directly under the shift lever, both unlocks and moves the raisable segment through simple supports. |
| [DE446800](../patents/DE446800.md) | 1927 | Nestler, Curt | A brush carried on a lever is moved by the type lever: it strokes the type on the way up, then swings clear on the return, so the type meets the brush only in motion. |
| [DE446801](../patents/DE446801.md) | 1927 |  | A plate with a felt pad is lifted by an oscillating shaft and link system, covering the type levers in the folded position. |
| [DE448293](../patents/DE448293.md) | 1927 |  | Each lever's pivot axis is not forced through the lever eye at right angles; each lever swings in the plane set by its own slot in the bow piece, so uniform slots and uniform lever bodies can be used without auxiliary bearings. |
| [DE448334](../patents/DE448334.md) | 1927 | Lehmann, Curt | A locking device for the basket simultaneously uncouples the key-lever connecting rods from the type levers by pivoting them, so the basket with its levers can be removed and replaced as a unit and reconnected safely. |
| [DE450249](../patents/DE450249.md) | 1927 |  | A plain U-section sheet box with no slots; the types are guided only by the end faces of the legs and a cover plate, so one box serves any type size. |
| [DE450288](../patents/DE450288.md) | 1927 | Stiebel, Drpl Theodor | A continuously rotating, motor-driven bead (cam-ridge) shaft is engaged by the depressed key lever and presses the type rod on through a simple push rod, and the same shaft also returns the key lever, so no return springs are needed. |
| [DE450914](../patents/DE450914.md) | 1927 | Pfeiffer, Otto | The intermediate links that drive the universal link attach directly to the type levers (through a second hole in the lever), giving more precise triggering, a lower segment position and fewer parts. |
| [DE451860](../patents/DE451860.md) | 1927 | Jacobsen, Johannes | The drawstring is looped through a slit at the foot of the type lever and unwinds along its curved edge, giving increasing acceleration; a rocker arm of the same length as the drawstring keeps the drive unchanged when the basket is switched. |
| [DE452925](../patents/DE452925.md) | 1927 |  | Key levers and angle levers are made as uniformly continuous parts, and the angle levers are carried on a continuous wire, so the linkage sits in a concentrated, uniform arrangement without lateral interference. |
| [DE453510](../patents/DE453510.md) | 1927 |  | A Johnsen-Rahbek relay (a rotating semiconductor roller with a conductive tape and a voltage source) is connected to a frame that mechanically couples the levers pressed by a key, so small control currents move the type levers with large force. |
| [DE453511](../patents/DE453511.md) | 1927 |  | A single small wing, only as high as one character, swings out on the snout of a liquid storage container against a spring; soaked with ink, it is struck by the returning type and inks only the character that hits it. |
| [DE453688](../patents/DE453688.md) | 1927 |  | A bent steel-wire spacer at the outer edge of the container snout acts as a sliding guide for the returning type, reducing friction and keeping the type's side faces clear of paint; a shut-off valve can limit paint escape. |
| [DE456355](../patents/DE456355.md) | 1928 |  | The guide blocks for the straight-moving type lever bearing segment are mounted on the intermediate lever bearing segment, which is screwed to the base plate, so the whole type lever unit is pre-assembled outside the frame and fixed with a few screws. |
| [DE457479](../patents/DE457479.md) | 1928 | Jacobsen, Johannes | A latch lever on the type lever closes a contact spring and slides along it, giving a fixed current time regardless of key travel. |
| [DE457482](../patents/DE457482.md) | 1928 |  | A one-piece guide with a slot extending nearly to its lower edge, joined by a narrow elastic web, lets the two legs be spread apart for smooth machining of each guide surface, then returned and set to the exact slot width with adjusting screws. |
| [DE457480](../patents/DE457480.md) | 1928 |  | Mount the shift frame, shift key levers and one end of the carriage compensation spring in a common support (pair of plates) that is adjustable as a unit relative to the main frame. |
| [DE460136](../patents/DE460136.md) | 1928 |  | Angle levers with rods drive each vertical type lever through a slightly overcentred joint, which locks it against recoil and allows a compact layout under the key levers. |
| [DE460998](../patents/DE460998.md) | 1928 |  | A light, thin-walled ink container is hung on grain tips at its centre of mass so it swings out to the type, reducing its moment of inertia while keeping the ink capacity. |
| [DE460999](../patents/DE460999.md) | 1928 |  | A small ink container feeds a felt inking pad drop by drop through a drip channel and hole above the highest liquid level, so only a little ink is needed. |
| [DE462096](../patents/DE462096.md) | 1928 | Liess, Andreas | A pressure plate that lies over all key levers and raises all typebars at once with one hand, leaving the other hand free to slide in a protective sheet. |
| [DE464939](../patents/DE464939.md) | 1928 |  | A thin wire is set into holes in the type bar guide cover, just proud of its face, so the bars land on the wire and do not strike the cover. |
| [DE465573](../patents/DE465573.md) | 1928 |  | A non-equal-armed angle lever engages the dead-centre intermediate link, and the type lever is driven through a pin and slot, which lets the type lever bearing sit in a plane nearly perpendicular to the key lever bearing. |
| [DE465574](../patents/DE465574.md) | 1928 |  | Switching the power source off sets each intermediate link back to rest, so keys pressed with the motor off cannot leave a type lever in the striking position. |
| [DE465928](../patents/DE465928.md) | 1928 |  | A tension spring is attached to the type lever and engages the type head through a semicircular rod, so that the line between the spring's two attachment points straddles the head's fulcrum in both end positions, forcing the head over and holding it in each. |
| [DE467043](../patents/DE467043.md) | 1928 | A, Ransmayer A & Rodrian | The ridge raised by embossing is kept and bent down to form the lateral fastening tabs, so the type body is shaped from a smaller ingot without the separate burr removal and slotting steps. |
| [DE467735](../patents/DE467735.md) | 1928 |  | Each type bar carries four types and is guided by two links, each pivoted by one of two keys from a four-row keyboard, so it reaches the print point by one of two paths. |
| [DE471256](../patents/DE471256.md) | 1929 |  | A harder buffer ring is rolled or pressed into a dovetail groove cut across the bearing body, perpendicular to the lever axis, so it is held on all sides with no fasteners. |
| [DE474863](../patents/DE474863.md) | 1929 |  | An intermediate double lever rolls on an upward curved rail, so the arm to the key lever shrinks and the arm to the type lever grows, giving a gentle start and a strong acceleration of the type lever. |
| [DE475673](../patents/DE475673.md) | 1929 | Kaeppler, Paul | A woven braided cord cushion, of cotton or metal, is held at its ends with sheet metal clips and catches the lever's fall; its elasticity lets the threads return so no permanent impressions form. |
| [DE476340](../patents/DE476340.md) | 1929 | Keining, Gustav | Characters are re-arranged on the type levers so each lever's three signs share a consonant and the e-group signs sit in the base position, so a and o signs need only one switch; the keyboard keeps its ordinary key count. |
| [DE477866](../patents/DE477866.md) | 1929 |  | One hand lever shifts the adding mechanism to the opposite calculation type through a rack and sliding block, and through push rods and a slotted arc rail it also blocks or releases the type levers. |
| [DE482201](../patents/DE482201.md) | 1929 | Liess, Andreas | A flat bar mounted above the key levers is turned a quarter turn with an outside handle, raising all type levers at once so a cleaning pad can be slid under them. |
| [DE483940](../patents/DE483940.md) | 1929 | Wallenstein, Alfred | The type lever is made thinner than the slot width, with grooves or spiral work bars formed at the bearing and type-block ends to give a fit in the slot and on the type block, leaving room for solder. |
| [DE483927](../patents/DE483927.md) | 1929 | D, Maschines A Ecrire Map Soc | A resilient tongue in the universal-rail linkage triggers the carriage step about a centimetre before the type hits the platen and then yields, letting the type lever finish. |
| [DE487475](../patents/DE487475.md) | 1929 |  | Type rods rest resiliently on the type hammers in rest position, and a return bracket swung out during cranking pivots the hammers into a preparatory position where the bracket is the stop, shortening the hammer stroke before release. |
| [DE491038](../patents/DE491038.md) | 1930 | Muelzel, Wilhelm | Each row has a pair of levers whose rear arcs are moved by the arithmetic unit from left to right; a stop lever holds the type carrier at zero, so the zero is set automatically in the writing mechanism. |
| [DE488212](../patents/DE488212.md) | 1930 |  | A continuously rotating type drum carries swinging type carriers in rows, and each carrier holds several types, so letters and numbers fit on a drum of the same size as the numeral-only machine; a card-controlled engagement element swings the chosen carrier to the platen abutment. |
| [DE493236](../patents/DE493236.md) | 1930 | Luce, Hans | The inventor changes the definition of the cutting tool (the saw blade) to keep the slot on course; the OCR is cut off before the exact method is stated. |
| [DE494712](../patents/DE494712.md) | 1930 | Luce, Hans | The lever cross-section is shaped so its smallest section falls on the true bend line, which lies on the centre plane of the type-lever segment. |
| [DE495880](../patents/DE495880.md) | 1930 | Luce, Hans | Each lever head is clamped in a fixture and its enlarged projected silhouette is adjusted against platen and baseline marks until correct, making the type-body position independent of the machine. |
| [DE496244](../patents/DE496244.md) | 1930 | Kaeppler, Paul | The rods are punched from unhardened wire with a tongue that bends over the lever bearing pin to retain the lever, and the rod is not hardened afterwards. |
| [DE496447](../patents/DE496447.md) | 1930 |  | Connecting rods are all the same length, but they grip different points on the intermediate and key levers of each row, and the drive units are assembled separately. |
| [DE498212](../patents/DE498212.md) | 1930 |  | A pawl on the intermediate lever cooperates with a stationary lug and stop and a leaf spring, so the drive parts vibrate rather than jerk, and the drive is disengaged in manual operation. |
| [DE498981](../patents/DE498981.md) | 1930 |  | Accent keys act through links on a second universal rail carried on a removable bracket, and a slotted swing arm drives the ribbon lifter from either rail. |
| [DE502799](../patents/DE502799.md) | 1930 | Coopera, Mondiale Typolitho Soc | The carriage advance is driven through a friction gear, so each key can give any spacing, set by the height of its stop on a shift rod. |
| [DE505405](../patents/DE505405.md) | 1930 | Joerissen, Carl A | Each type lever is driven by a pin from the key, so the type stops and the key then moves it into print position, with a fixed pivot. |
| [DE505802](../patents/DE505802.md) | 1930 | Tauschek, Gustav | Stencil levers carry character cut-outs; pressing a key opens a valve so compressed-air paint passes through the cut-out onto the paper, with no ribbon. |
| [DE506060](../patents/DE506060.md) | 1930 |  | Bores in the guide parts hold oil-soaked wicks fed from small oil containers, so passing type levers are oiled. |
| [DE507696](../patents/DE507696.md) | 1930 | Steiner, Fritz | Typebar levers strike at a fixed stop on the platen while rake-type rods are guided in a bearing, with multiplier setting wheels and cam disks driven from the arithmetic keys. |
| [DE508828](../patents/DE508828.md) | 1930 | W, Mueller U V Nemecek Fa | A pressure roller on the lever's pin replaces the sector-shaped pressure piece, and the drawstring has a length and a slot that let it release if caught. |
| [DE509268](../patents/DE509268.md) | 1930 |  | A single horizontal double-armed intermediate lever with its pivot in the segment extension replaces the angle lever and wire links. |
| [DE510204](../patents/DE510204.md) | 1930 | Maschf, Rheinische Metallw & | An assembly frame clamped to the type lever head holds a slide with a micrometer screw that sets the type body position. |
| [DE513129](../patents/DE513129.md) | 1930 |  | Pins on the key levers slide along horizontal guide edges of the intermediate levers, shortening the drive lever arm for faster stop acceleration. |
| [DE516463](../patents/DE516463.md) | 1931 |  | Rows of adjustable stops sit on a common movable box; a shift key sets a switching stop on a double-armed lever, and the stops are cleared when the box returns. |
| [DE519544](../patents/DE519544.md) | 1931 |  | A link hinged to the key lever with an elongated hole around the intermediate member lets the key lever and the intermediate member stay engaged, with the type lever return spring independent of the key spring. |
| [DE520230](../patents/DE520230.md) | 1931 | Kaeppler, Paul | A fixed optical sight with a marking on its field shows the exact location and direction where a letter should appear if it matches a well-aligned type, giving an objective check independent of the operator. |
| [DE521878](../patents/DE521878.md) | 1931 |  | A hardened and ground steel ball is pressed into a hole at the impact point of the type lever, protruding slightly on both sides to run in the type lever guide, with the guide spacing matched to the ball diameter. |
| [DE521879](../patents/DE521879.md) | 1931 | Roth, Franz | Double-sided type bars carried in a frame that moves along and across the platen are struck by one hammer whose upward or downward stroke picks the upper or lower type. |
| [DE521880](../patents/DE521880.md) | 1931 |  | The engaging part of the pawl swings on the rest of the pawl against its own spring, limited by a pin in an enlarged bore, so the return spring is independent of the pawl engagement part. |
| [DE522484](../patents/DE522484.md) | 1931 | Wallenstein, Alfred | Key and intermediate levers sit in superimposed horizontal slots with pivots on a larger circle, so all slots lie in the same planes and can be cut in one saw pass, with type levers swinging over 90 degrees. |
| [DE518524](../patents/DE518524.md) | 1931 | Winterling, Carl | Two rows of typebars share the same comb guides and drive, and differently curved bars bring both rows to the common print point. |
| [DE525926](../patents/DE525926.md) | 1931 | Holzer, Rudolf | A hand-held pen-shaped stylus moves a type selector in three dimensions through a zigzag-edged opening in a pivoted setting plate, so one motion selects the type lever on a disc and then fires the hammer. |
| [DE528588](../patents/DE528588.md) | 1931 |  | The handlebar joins the key lever to the push rod through a downward slotted guide, resiliently held by a spring and moved by a tab of the button lever, so the push rod moves without oscillation. |
| [DE529228](../patents/DE529228.md) | 1931 |  | The segment sits on a stamped cross-shaped switching frame with a variable-angle adjusting screw in its lower web, the frame is suspended in three points on a rocking plate, and stop pins set the upper and lower positions. |
| [DE533697](../patents/DE533697.md) | 1931 |  | Give the write keys and shift keys a common pivot axis and connect each shift key to the type-lever carrier by pin and slot, saving a pivot axis, springs and levers. |
| [DE533943](../patents/DE533943.md) | 1931 |  | Mount each lifting disc with its holding and pressure levers in a self-contained frame of two angled plates, detachably carried on a bearing part on the machine's cross rails. |
| [DE535361](../patents/DE535361.md) | 1931 |  | Each key group has only one shift key, leaving seven character keys and two vowel keys, with a shared number shift across the whole keyboard. |
| [DE535363](../patents/DE535363.md) | 1931 |  | Directional projections are placed in a recess of the type body, and the embossing die has a matching elevation with milled depressions. The alignment projections sit in the recess and do not reach the printed impression. |
| [DE535861](../patents/DE535861.md) | 1931 |  | The sliding drive member is mounted in the fixed frame, and the pin and slot connection is between the sliding member and the parts it drives, so the shifted segment needs only light pivot bearings. |
| [DE536814](../patents/DE536814.md) | 1931 |  | Give each catch lever a second catch nose above the first, so a held key stops the disc after one stroke. |
| [DE537963](../patents/DE537963.md) | 1931 |  | A device excludes actuation of the writing mechanism by the numeric keys when the arithmetic mechanism is active, by driving the numeric writing keys from arithmetic members so that the keypad stays in place and cannot be pressed. |
| [DE538090](../patents/DE538090.md) | 1931 | Ets, Continsouza Soc | The return spring acts on a threaded slider nut on a partly threaded spindle; the spindle is turned from the front and a spring keeps the nut pressed back onto the thread. |
| [DE539706](../patents/DE539706.md) | 1931 | Weissfloch, Konrad Karl | The type and key levers are carried on horizontal parallel bearings in a housing that slides parallel to the platen, so each type lever can be brought into the printing position; the levers can be spaced wide to take large types. |
| [DE540137](../patents/DE540137.md) | 1931 | Luce, Hans | The typebar is clamped in a jig with bending edges and a long extension piece, so the head bends to a fixed correct angle and position with spring-back allowed for. |
| [DE542875](../patents/DE542875.md) | 1932 |  | A widened typebar head carries several type blocks side by side, each individually interchangeable without tools, so a whole date prints with one stroke. |
| [DE544901](../patents/DE544901.md) | 1932 | Keining, Gustav | Put consonant signs on the type levers already combined with the following e-group vowel, with a and o vowels on separate types, so no shift is needed. |
| [DE544902](../patents/DE544902.md) | 1932 |  | Each type body has alignment notches on its sides at a fixed distance from the baseline, cut at the same time as the burr trimming; two opposite points engage the notches to align the type on the type lever. |
| [DE546834](../patents/DE546834.md) | 1932 | Oberholzer, Frithjof | A locking lever on the machine frame puts a line-type lever in working position and couples the ribbon spool to the carriage drive, so pressing the tab or carriage release key draws a continuous line. |
| [DE547910](../patents/DE547910.md) | 1932 |  | Support each type bar's frame member at two widely separated points so that one allows adjustment in the strike direction (impression) and the other allows vertical adjustment, independently. |
| [DE547909](../patents/DE547909.md) | 1932 |  | Each sliding type bar is mounted in its own carrier, pivotable as a whole, so every carrier can be made and adjusted separately. |
| [DE549139](../patents/DE549139.md) | 1932 |  | Each key-lever motion reaches the vertical type lever through an angle lever and intermediate lever whose hinge points are placed so the rest position is over-centre (self-locking) and the first motion accelerates gradually. |
| [DE549650](../patents/DE549650.md) | 1932 | Arondel, Maurice Leon | Use the machine's existing universal (stop) rail, carried along mechanically by the keys, as the contact breaker that closes the electromagnet circuit, so the magnet completes the type stroke after a light key tap. |
| [DE551030](../patents/DE551030.md) | 1932 |  | The intermediate-lever bearing is made vertically adjustable so it can be set to the segment after the segment is adjusted. |
| [DE551515](../patents/DE551515.md) | 1932 |  | A pivoted carrier holds the sliding type bar; a ratchet device on the constantly turning power shaft moves it, and a pin-and-slot locking lever swings the carrier when the second key is pressed. |
| [DE552609](../patents/DE552609.md) | 1932 |  | Two scissor-like interconnected pawl parts in different horizontal planes hold the typebar, which lets it sit directly above the key levers, and an arm on the intermediate lever blocks further keys until the typebar returns. |
| [DE552835](../patents/DE552835.md) | 1932 | Tauschek, Gustav | Two rows of spring contacts, overlapping like roof tiles, form a circuit that closes only when a single key is pressed; two keys open the circuit. |
| [DE556270](../patents/DE556270.md) | 1932 |  | A pivoted lever normally engaged with the guide member tilts it positively, and is disengaged when the other key is pressed. |
| [DE556956](../patents/DE556956.md) | 1932 | Bloch, Grigory K Block | The type lever returns to rest through the paper carriage switching device, with pawls on a ratchet and a snap-release rod, so no extra spring is needed. |
| [DE557116](../patents/DE557116.md) | 1932 |  | Upper and lower ball tracks on the frame and segment, with one side adjustable on racks, keep the segment square with little free play. |
| [DE557522](../patents/DE557522.md) | 1932 | Wolff, Robert | The whole type guide, or its guide legs, is adjustable in height and along the type body so each lever strikes a fresh guide point. |
| [DE557652](../patents/DE557652.md) | 1932 |  | Mount angled type levers on upright guide rods and drive rods whose pivots are arranged so the type slows to a stop at the platen, and add rolling key-lever linkages that delay the final motion. |
| [DE558156](../patents/DE558156.md) | 1932 | Geb, Merz | Type levers stand vertically in front of the platen sector and are swung by concentric intermediate levers, tie rods and angle levers to the key bars. |
| [DE558571](../patents/DE558571.md) | 1932 |  | Eccentric drives that are coupled in dead-centre positions move the type bars gradually, so the striking force is higher and quiet. |
| [DE560695](../patents/DE560695.md) | 1932 |  | Each type lever swings to a horizontal position in front of the platen and carries a sliding type block, which a common pressure member in the segment centre then pushes straight against the platen. |
| [DE562670](../patents/DE562670.md) | 1932 |  | Type levers on a rotating drum are driven by separate cam bodies, shaped for the desired blow strength at any drum speed, instead of by hook stops catching the levers themselves. |
| [DE563630](../patents/DE563630.md) | 1932 | Avon, Giovanni | A spring-struck type lever is returned to rest by an electromagnet whose armature acts on a universal rod that also controls the magnet's own switch. |
| [DE564219](../patents/DE564219.md) | 1932 | Lewin, Awram | Mount the key set on a slider, with keys whose heads span two type levers, so shifting the slider makes the keys strike either one type set or both at once. |
| [DE564326](../patents/DE564326.md) | 1932 |  | A second shift rail with cutouts and projections, linked to selected key levers, swings an auxiliary stop into the path of the movable escapement pawl so that those letters give only a single step even in the upper platen position. |
| [DE565253](../patents/DE565253.md) | 1932 | Gruetzmann, Paul | The type lever swings then slides straight on a stationary flat axle through two guide slots, with a resilient bumper stop. |
| [DE566606](../patents/DE566606.md) | 1932 |  | Strike the type lever by the typist's finger as usual, and power only the carriage step and ribbon lifter, triggered by a universal rail that the type lever moves at the end of its stroke. |
| [DE567401](../patents/DE567401.md) | 1933 |  | Each type lever carries several adjustable types, and a second selector sets the wanted type into the printing position as the lever passes. |
| [DE568434](../patents/DE568434.md) | 1933 |  | The intermediate levers slide on a backward-curved bar of the key-lever guide comb, guided by upturned tongues, and springs hold them on the comb and return the key and type levers. |
| [DE568677](../patents/DE568677.md) | 1933 | Denzer, Ludwig | The type cushion is an elastic rubber strip with a trapezoidal recess that clamps onto its bracket, with projections and longitudinal grooves that create an air gap for damping and catch dust for easy removal. |
| [DE568790](../patents/DE568790.md) | 1933 |  | A resilient plate on a swing-out support damps the initial movement and speeds the final movement of each type lever through a front intermediate lever; the intermediate lever yields to the power lever and is returned by a spring before the type lever reaches the platen. |
| [DE569299](../patents/DE569299.md) | 1933 |  | The multi-step preparatory jumper is placed in the removable type lever basket while the members it actuates stay in the machine frame, so swapping the basket changes the spacing behaviour. |
| [DE569451](../patents/DE569451.md) | 1933 |  | Each type bar is driven by a toggle lever with two independent control angle levers connected by tie rods to the power drive, one of which also acts on the pivoting carrier. |
| [DE570162](../patents/DE570162.md) | 1933 |  | All key levers carry an identical arc-shaped nose with an elongated hole for the lower end of the pull rod, and the intermediate-lever suspension points are placed so the tie rods hang in one plane, so one part shape serves all levers. |
| [DE571525](../patents/DE571525.md) | 1933 | Goerwitz, Oskar | Each type rocker arm is hinged to a stretching lever system so that, on the way from rest to standby, centrifugal force adds to the lever action and presses the type against the paper roller without a striking sound; a sliding type carrier carries several rows of types (for example Latin and Russian). |
| [DE572644](../patents/DE572644.md) | 1933 |  | Both legs of the auxiliary knee lever are hinged to a lug on the main toggle arm below the main toggle joint, and the main toggle is fixed to the frame at the pressure point so the force runs straight through the type lever to the paper roller. |
| [DE573871](../patents/DE573871.md) | 1933 |  | A standard type lever carries two interchangeable type bodies (day and month) as a fork-guided pair held on the lever head by a locking member, so ordinary levers can be used and the date types are quick to change. |
| [DE573908](../patents/DE573908.md) | 1933 |  | An arm on the pawl carrier is joined to the pawl by a spring whose pull line crosses the axis, so it holds the pawl in engagement until the arm hits a stop that flips the spring back. |
| [DE573910](../patents/DE573910.md) | 1933 |  | Type levers rotate on an arc around a fixed centre and are pressed noiselessly onto the paper roller by a toggle joint; an auxiliary knee-lever joint is stretched in the rest position and bent only when the key is struck, so the stroke is light and the type is held against rebound. |
| [DE573913](../patents/DE573913.md) | 1933 | Eibert, Fritz | The eccentric guide members are shaped so they contact each type lever roughly at the middle of its side just before the platen, where the lever is not worn, keeping the impression even even when the levers are worn. |
| [DE575861](../patents/DE575861.md) | 1933 | Eckert, Eduard | The type lever is formed as a tube with a collar at its end and is guided on pins by fork-like caps, with an unequal-leg fork in front of the platen to stop each lever at the ink pad or platen. |
| [DE576078](../patents/DE576078.md) | 1933 | A, Ransmayer A & Rodrian | Wedge-shaped alignment projections are placed at the ends of the type body, lower than the types, and the body is set on the lever against a gauge with matching recesses so every body sits at the same distance from the baseline. |
| [DE576188](../patents/DE576188.md) | 1933 | Gruetzmann, Paul | Adjacent type levers are pivoted so one hangs with its head down and the next stands upright, letting them swing from opposite directions to a closer, shorter bearing arrangement that strikes the platen squarely. |
| [DE576189](../patents/DE576189.md) | 1933 |  | The type carrier stays permanently coupled to a crank, and the crank passes through a stretched toggle at printing, so the type presses on the platen quietly and the drive returns smoothly on a single rotation direction. |
| [DE576534](../patents/DE576534.md) | 1933 |  | The swing weight is placed below the impression point and engages the type lever directly through a pin and slot, so it brakes the lever near the end of the keystroke without intermediate linkages. |
| [DE576533](../patents/DE576533.md) | 1933 |  | A pin on the type lever runs along a thrust cam on the swing weight, so the weight is accelerated smoothly, brakes the lever, and returns its stored energy when the type reaches the platen. |
| [DE576313](../patents/DE576313.md) | 1933 | Benzing, Hugo | Each friction rod is moved by an adjustable intermediate lever on a spring-loaded eccentric, released by the key lever and returned by the type-lever action, so the rod briefly presses on continuously running rollers for one stop. |
| [DE579491](../patents/DE579491.md) | 1933 |  | The auxiliary toggle points are aligned in a straight line at rest so the keystroke starts slowly, the swing weight's centre of gravity supports the initial motion and the return, and the return movement of the swing weight is not stopped by any limit. |
| [DE579749](../patents/DE579749.md) | 1933 | Grah, Ernst | Make the type rods star-shaped so they touch the guide tube only along narrow edges, driven by low-pressure air. |
| [DE580200](../patents/DE580200.md) | 1933 | A, Ransmayer A & Rodrian | The jig uses only a few recesses matched to the type height, with adjustable retaining jaws on either side of the lever's plane of symmetry that hold the body in its correct lateral position. |
| [DE580286](../patents/DE580286.md) | 1933 | A, Ransmayer A & Rodrian | The adjusting projection is a separate shoulder piece on the type body, used for alignment during soldering and then removed, leaving a body of the usual length. |
| [DE582391](../patents/DE582391.md) | 1933 |  | A single sliding lock bar with prongs and cutouts alternately locks the letter keys or the figure keys, while leaving the two-character keys free, and it is moved by the figure shift key. |
| [DE585280](../patents/DE585280.md) | 1933 | Rosenthal, Hellmuth | Drive the type carrier with two toggle-lever pairs that reach their straight positions at print and at rest, absorbing the shock, with a guide piece sliding on a straight rod. |
| [DE587130](../patents/DE587130.md) | 1933 |  | The drive arm of each type lever pivots as one part of a toggle linkage whose other joint is carried by the segment; the knee extends at impact to stop rebound and gives a long lever arm at the start of the stroke. |
| [DE590368](../patents/DE590368.md) | 1934 | Joerissen, Carl A | The articulation point of the holding rod can be moved by hand to one of four positions, so the type stops just short of the platen, making the machine quiet; in the other positions it strikes normally. |
| [DE591100](../patents/DE591100.md) | 1934 |  | A small device clamped to the type lever inside the basket sets the baseline of the old type and then holds the new type in an adjustable alignment and clamping fixture for soldering. |
| [DE592208](../patents/DE592208.md) | 1934 |  | Type levers are arranged in radial planes with their drive rods and rockers, the key levers are aligned along a straight support rod, and each rocker sits on its own bearing block hooked into radial slots of the frame plate, giving equal transmission for all keys. |
| [DE592587](../patents/DE592587.md) | 1934 | Wolff, Robert | A carrier held in a vice has two fixed pressure inserts and one movable insert; a spindle through the carrier drives the movable insert, so the deflection and the lengthening or shortening of the type lever can be set exactly. |
| [DE592588](../patents/DE592588.md) | 1934 | Wolff, Robert | A clamp insert with a notch for the lever neck and a recess matching the type head, plus pressure bolts, lets the type be shifted sideways to a set position in a vice. |
| [DE595105](../patents/DE595105.md) | 1934 | Simson, Donald Petrie | Each toothed type bar has a spring-loaded angle lever whose catch engages a tooth, and a second spring-loaded lever then pushes the bar gently toward the paper roller so the types stop in a straight line without impact. |
| [DE597818](../patents/DE597818.md) | 1934 | Loewenbuck, Sebastian | Horizontally sliding type rods are driven by horizontally sliding key rods through gears, giving a flat machine. |
| [DE603551](../patents/DE603551.md) | 1934 |  | A stationary platen with a light circular type-lever basket on a rotating spindle, with the selector mechanism moved inside the basket. |
| [DE604492](../patents/DE604492.md) | 1934 |  | One drive part moves out to set the counter and back to drive the number type, so printing can only follow counter setting. |
| [DE607152](../patents/DE607152.md) | 1934 |  | Rear intermediate levers sit on a common straight fixed shaft, graduated in length so the rear link travel is equal for all levers in both segment positions. |
| [DE607463](../patents/DE607463.md) | 1934 |  | Key levers are mounted on straight axes, and the distances of tension member joints on type levers scale in the same ratio as those on the lower links. |
| [DE607965](../patents/DE607965.md) | 1935 | Schuller, Walter | Each type carries three characters on a double shift, so the three characters stand at top, main and bottom lines. Vowels are shown partly by height (two heights) and partly by stroke weight (three weights from bevelled type flanks), and keys have a small and a broad striking surface so the force used changes the impression. |
| [DE609933](../patents/DE609933.md) | 1935 |  | Type levers on a stationary pin are swung into the printing position by toggle levers with slotted guides, and the type block pressure element is then brought onto the platen. |
| [DE610101](../patents/DE610101.md) | 1935 | A, Ransmayer A & Rodrian | Each type body is aligned by pressing one character edge against a fixed stop bar of the setting device, so no per-type marks are needed. |
| [DE610662](../patents/DE610662.md) | 1935 |  | Use a freely turning star wheel with a row of fingers on the power lever so each power-shaft tooth engages two fingers in toggle fashion, with no idle return motion. |
| [DE611058](../patents/DE611058.md) | 1935 | Wolff, Robert | A tool with a U-shaped support held in a vice carries a spring-loaded pressure bolt that presses the typebar foot, and a wedge locks the block so the typebar foot can be widened by tightening the vice. |
| [DE617939](../patents/DE617939.md) | 1935 | Noltenius, Friedrich | Make the type rod in two jointed sections so the front, type-bearing part drops out of the printing plane on return and frees the way for the next type. |
| [DE618376](../patents/DE618376.md) | 1935 | Noltenius, Friedrich | Drive each type carrier by a flywheel that the key turns 180-360 degrees one way only, using its inertia to press toggle-like and then move on to a second rest position. |
| [DE619349](../patents/DE619349.md) | 1935 |  | Use piston-and-cylinder pneumatic drives to return both carriage and writing device simultaneously from one switching device. |
| [DE619740](../patents/DE619740.md) | 1935 | Benzing, Hugo | The rocker-arm attack points for each row sit at distances matched to key-lever length, so all type-lever drives share the same amplitude, acceleration and force through tie rods. |
| [DE622361](../patents/DE622361.md) | 1935 |  | Two concentric shift rails both act on one common lever that drives the ribbon lift, while only the ordinary rail releases the carriage escapement. |
| [DE624624](../patents/DE624624.md) | 1936 | Haas, Otto | The segment's top face is bevelled so its edges are two eccentric circular arcs, making the guide slots deepest at the outside and shallowest in the middle. |
| [DE627746](../patents/DE627746.md) | 1936 | Kissner, Erika | Individual special characters, including an upright hexagon, are added to the type set so they can be struck in sequence with ordinary characters using the existing switching means, without changing the lever count. |
