| [DE632323](../patents/DE632323.md) | 1936 | Mansel, Paul | The type levers sit in a basket sleeve that rotates and slides axially on a fixed tube, so an axial move selects a ring of levers and rotation picks one; a single central actuator presses the selected lever radially through a slot. |
| [DE632573](../patents/DE632573.md) | 1936 |  | Limit the return of each type carrier and linkage with stops on a common stop rail, while each carrier's bearing parts are adjustable. |
| [DE633145](../patents/DE633145.md) | 1936 | Paul, Arnold | Each key lever gets several stops and counter-stops on the switching bridge, and the bridge is turned about its axis to bring the set needed for that character into use, so each letter switches the carriage by its own width. |
| [DE636150](../patents/DE636150.md) | 1936 | Winterling, Carl | Guide each typebar on its own fixed, polished guard rail with fork-like fingers on the bar, so the front guide can be moved right up to about 40 mm from the print point. |
| [DE636946](../patents/DE636946.md) | 1936 | Sl, Erga | All note characters sit as type levers on one circular type basket, and a single keyboard of as many keys as pitch heights moves a vertically displaceable type-lever carrier slide so the attachment point of the type lever shifts to the required pitch; a scale-rail adjuster sets the basket position. |
| [DE639372](../patents/DE639372.md) | 1936 | Zschimmer, Moritz | Before soldering each type to its lever, the paper-roller stop point is measured in a work frame, a replacement roller body is set to that measured position, and the types are aligned with adjustable marks against it and then soldered. |
| [DE639714](../patents/DE639714.md) | 1936 |  | A pointer linked to the spring holder moves inside the frame and shows the spring tension on a scale visible through a window, while a separate adjuster changes the tension. |
| [DE640321](../patents/DE640321.md) | 1936 | Winterling, Carl | Type lever, intermediate lever and key lever of each drive all pivot in one radial slot of a single conical-sector bearing body, so assembly errors cancel. |
| [DE645557](../patents/DE645557.md) | 1937 |  | Use a very thin (about 0.2 mm) watch-spring steel plate screwed only on the platen side, loosely resting on the guide frame on the other side. |
| [DE645558](../patents/DE645558.md) | 1937 | Typewriters, L C Smith & Corona | Intermediate levers are linked to the key levers by a fixed connection and a spring acts through an intermediate piece, so the type lever returns in a straight line without bounce. |
| [DE645610](../patents/DE645610.md) | 1937 |  | A separate date type lever sits in a bearing on the outside of the type basket, is driven by a power drive under key control, and carries interchangeable day, month and year types. |
| [DE645972](../patents/DE645972.md) | 1937 |  | Letter carriers are driven without a dwell at the reversal points, while number carriers keep the dwell for adding wheels. |
| [DE646341](../patents/DE646341.md) | 1937 | Hieb, Alois | Key levers are pivoted on a circular arc concentric with the intermediate levers' bearing arc, with a cranked pull on the intermediate lever, so the machine is compact. |
| [DE639663](../patents/DE639663.md) | 1937 | Stefan, Jakoblits | The calculator's forward drive moves the platen closer to the type carrier, so the typebar has only a short movement during printing. |
| [DE648238](../patents/DE648238.md) | 1937 | Baier, Georg | All push and movement members sit above and below the rear trajectory of the type carrier guide rod, so the drive is short and fits in the space the guide rod used. |
| [DE648239](../patents/DE648239.md) | 1937 |  | Couple both the bar-driving members and the group selector to a power drive, with the group set by spring force and the return by the power drive storing that spring force. |
| [DE649546](../patents/DE649546.md) | 1937 | Benzing, Hugo | Free ends of the rocker arm are rigidly connected to the key levers, so the rocker stays in contact with the rolling surface in every position. |
| [DE649679](../patents/DE649679.md) | 1937 | A, Ransmayer A & Rodrian | A gauge whose carrier is clamped to the platen on a steel band, with a sliding guide part adjusted lengthwise, so that replacement types can be soldered to the typebar with alignment marks matched to a well-preserved type. |
| [DE649759](../patents/DE649759.md) | 1937 |  | Two intermediate levers, one giving normal acceleration and one a second boost via pins in slots of the typebar, change engagement at a set point. |
| [DE652847](../patents/DE652847.md) | 1937 | Baier, Georg | The upper angled drive member works through a straight slot in a radius from the fulcrum, and a swing weight lever links it to the typebar. |
| [DE653587](../patents/DE653587.md) | 1937 |  | Mount the line-height pointer and ribbon lifter on parts independent of the in-and-out adjustment of the platen carriage, so they keep a constant distance from the type guide. |
| [DE754784](../patents/DE754784.md) | 1938 |  | Mount each type lever independently of other parts and let a shared segment-shaped electromagnet accelerate the lever through iron lamellae fixed to it, switched by the key lever. |
| [DE658285](../patents/DE658285.md) | 1938 |  | Raise the type lever with a continuously coupled eccentric drive and then press its head to the platen with a rotating pressure cam near the print point. |
| [DE662539](../patents/DE662539.md) | 1938 | Hieb, Alois | An extension bent from the key lever, ending in a bent-over guide loop, acts directly on the intermediate lever carrying the type bar. |
| [DE663665](../patents/DE663665.md) | 1938 |  | The type-bar or setting-lever connections are made with a flat joint, borrowed from telegraph punches and held by a leaf spring, so several bars fit side by side at a smaller pitch. |
| [DE666766](../patents/DE666766.md) | 1938 |  | Type outer sides are angled so clamp jaws can press it toward the base at any clamp direction. |
| [DE666896](../patents/DE666896.md) | 1938 | Tappen, Hans | Design the shapes and placement of the type characters so a wrong character can be overprinted with a second character in the same position and be converted invisibly into the correct one, with a fine lateral adjustment of the type or stop point where needed. |
| [DE667442](../patents/DE667442.md) | 1938 | J, Ludwig Neumann | Gauge carrier with clamping devices grips any type lever without changing it. |
| [DE668229](../patents/DE668229.md) | 1938 |  | Rest bearing has collecting surfaces on the sides of the pressure elements, so only the sides touch and the back does not strike. |
| [DE668412](../patents/DE668412.md) | 1938 |  | Mount the shift levers, universal bar, carriage bracket and space bar on two plain bright steel wire axles pushed through bearing eyes in an injection-moulded base plate and held in place by the sheet-metal casing. |
| [DE669542](../patents/DE669542.md) | 1938 | Benzing, Hugo | Levers sit between the legs of a fork with opposing adjusting screws, tapered to remove play. |
| [DE669731](../patents/DE669731.md) | 1939 |  | The control bar is rigidly coupled to the drive shaft so no spring is needed for the print stroke, with strong return springs and an anti-rebound device to catch the type lever. |
| [DE756612](../patents/DE756612.md) | 1939 | Bromund, Arthur | A motor-driven gear sits between the drive member and power source; pressing a key couples the type lever's drive linkage to the mechanical drive and it uncouples automatically after the imprint, even with the key still held. |
| [DE671168](../patents/DE671168.md) | 1939 |  | Gears slow the type's final approach to the roller, and the roller shifts briefly sideways so the impact happens at low relative speed; the roller's inertia supplies the impression force. |
| [DE672394](../patents/DE672394.md) | 1939 |  | Type rods that carry both a special character and a zero are driven by coupling members so that zeros print in the right places and special characters never appear where they are not wanted. |
| [DE672436](../patents/DE672436.md) | 1939 |  | Key-lever bearing points are set at heights that follow the curvature of the type-lever bearing rows, with one bearing column per key, so the key levers drive the type levers directly. |
| [DE673243](../patents/DE673243.md) | 1939 | Hediger, Arthur | A lever mechanism keeps the driver for the type lever engaged after a zero key is struck, and the counter's control rail influences that lever so the zeros stop at the number's end. |
| [DE673539](../patents/DE673539.md) | 1939 | E, Paillard & Cie Sa | Multi-armed intermediate levers whose arms are the sole adjusting members for the type lever and for two universal rails, one for carriage escapement and one for ribbon lift. |
| [DE675234](../patents/DE675234.md) | 1939 |  | A pivoting directional piece engages the slot of the type body and the type guide head to set lateral alignment; the type body is then clamped in place and the alignment members removed before soldering. |
| [DE675413](../patents/DE675413.md) | 1939 | Denzer, Ludwig | A resilient strip, rubber-based, in a rigid support bracket absorbs the type lever on its rest, reducing noise and sagging. |
| [DE675676](../patents/DE675676.md) | 1939 | Benzing, Hugo | A hose body of braided rubber thread with knitted fabric or web is elastic transversely so only a few threads take each hit, and the threads spring back without disturbing neighbours. |
| [DE678185](../patents/DE678185.md) | 1939 | Piltz, Waldemar | One shared electromagnet drives all type levers; each type lever has a rigid segment extension acting as the armature, so only a light mass moves. |
| [DE678362](../patents/DE678362.md) | 1939 |  | The power drive itself both moves the type-group selecting members into position and returns them, with the typebars thrown to print by the same drive. |
| [DE679177](../patents/DE679177.md) | 1939 |  | The eccentric drive pulleys are moved to the rear of the machine next to the link system, and the release pawl sits at the end of an extended key lever, which also gives noise damping. |
| [DE679178](../patents/DE679178.md) | 1939 |  | An eccentric, freely rotating pressure roller engages the type-lever head near the end of its path and presses it against the platen, so the stroke is cushioned and pressure is applied at the impression point. |
| [DE679179](../patents/DE679179.md) | 1939 |  | The raceway of the eccentric drive pulley guides an intermediate lever through blocks or rollers on two opposite sides, so the linkage is positively driven in both directions without springs. |
| [DE679758](../patents/DE679758.md) | 1939 |  | Separate power drives for writing and punching; the key-lever coupling engages the punch drive, and the punch's own power-drive members release the card-carriage inhibitor on retraction. |
| [DE680880](../patents/DE680880.md) | 1939 |  | Before hardening, guide parts are pressed between a punch and a die whose faces carry small grain-like raised points, exceeding the elastic limit so the alignment is permanent; the resulting lines do not harm the sanded guide surfaces. |
| [DE681051](../patents/DE681051.md) | 1939 |  | A resilient L-shaped intermediate member placed in a recess of the key lever lets the power-lever extension enter and be locked, so a held key does not repeat the strike. |
| [DE681197](../patents/DE681197.md) | 1939 | Typewriters, L C Smith & Corona | Carriage and type-lever linkage sit on a subframe that is elastically suspended on the main frame and can swing in all directions; the key levers stay on the main frame. |
| [DE681271](../patents/DE681271.md) | 1939 | Schuller, Walter | The types are mounted individually on the type bodies, each moved by a linkage from its own part of a split key, so the upper, middle and lower signs reach their line without any shift. |
| [DE681272](../patents/DE681272.md) | 1939 | Mansel, Paul | Carriage feed is built up from binary-weighted basic values added in a multiple differential gear, with a counting device adding extra feed per letter or space in a second pass. |
| [DE631723](../patents/DE631723.md) | 1939 |  | Each type bar, carrying two types and driven by its own key, hangs on two links forming a four-bar linkage and is thrown to the platen by inertia along a near straight short path. |
| [DE632552](../patents/DE632552.md) | 1939 |  | Each typebar carries two type bodies; its front and rear links are placed so the middle and rear of the bar swing in an arc while the type face moves nearly straight to the platen. |
| [DE683418](../patents/DE683418.md) | 1939 | Roth, Franz | An inclined type-lever segment carries the middle group driven directly by its key levers; the outer groups are driven by intermediate levers with adjustable bearing pins so all keys have equal stroke. |
| [DE687973](../patents/DE687973.md) | 1940 |  | An interchangeable auxiliary device bends the type-lever head and sets the type angle and distance from the lever axis. |
| [DE687974](../patents/DE687974.md) | 1940 |  | The stop and the type support curved to the platen are adjustable perpendicular to the baseline in the alignment plane, so every type group sits correctly at the print point. |
| [DE754374](../patents/DE754374.md) | 1940 |  | Bearing arms and a bearing block on the segment carrier adjust the link pivots so the switch bracket is parallel to the type-lever axis. |
| [DE691711](../patents/DE691711.md) | 1940 |  | Superposed steel sheets with sound-absorbing layers between them increase in rigidity with distance from the lever, so contact resistance grows gradually with contact area. |
| [DE693974](../patents/DE693974.md) | 1940 | Comp, Underwood Elliott Fischer | A cord of long-fiber asbestos is spun into yarn, twisted into threads and a cord laid inside an abrasion-resistant fabric sheath, forming a rest pad with silent, rebound-free stops. |
| [DE695069](../patents/DE695069.md) | 1940 | Zinter, Richard | The type block slides onto a slideway at the typebar head from below and is held by a pawl-like retaining spring snapping into a locking groove, with a fixed stop above. |
| [DE697152](../patents/DE697152.md) | 1940 | Maier, Lorenz | Calculating type carriers are moved by linkage rods driven by a cam, so they rest below the print line and print at the same point as the typewriter type bars on the platen. |
| [DE697794](../patents/DE697794.md) | 1940 |  | The energy store that continues the type lever after the key stops is an eccentric swing weight serving as the articulation in a toggle linkage, giving a strong, even, quiet impression. |
| [DE698103](../patents/DE698103.md) | 1940 |  | A step piece only pivots to select the positive or negative group of characters, and a pawl on the swivel arm sets the sign position from the counter's subtraction slider. |
| [DE698373](../patents/DE698373.md) | 1940 | Gerber, Paul | Rest pad is felt with an elastic rubber-thread fabric layer and a woollen cover where the type lever strikes. |
| [DE700501](../patents/DE700501.md) | 1940 | Vogt, Alfred | Keep all drive links permanently hinged, with a knee joint between the swing-weight drive lever and the double lever from the key lever, so both link groups can be adjusted independently. |
| [DE701180](../patents/DE701180.md) | 1941 |  | The tail profile is rolled or drawn as a continuous bar, blanks are cut off transversely, and the head with its character is embossed in one step. |
| [DE701372](../patents/DE701372.md) | 1941 | A, C Olivetti & C S | A crank linkage hinged to a fixed machine axis rotates the type on its lever as the lever swings, with near-zero turning speed at the start and no load on the type. |
| [DE701937](../patents/DE701937.md) | 1941 |  | A fixture holds the type lever segment on a base; the lever is pivoted in and pressed against its bearing, and the type is held on a die with an alignment notch by electromagnet. |
| [DE702111](../patents/DE702111.md) | 1941 |  | A U-shaped sheet metal drive rod whose legs are pressed together along the part not at risk of buckling, giving rigidity and damped vibration. |
| [DE753629](../patents/DE753629.md) | 1941 |  | Make bearing and joint points elastic via cut-out resilient arms and add sound-absorbing plastic layers so impact vibration is damped before reaching the frame. |

| [DE705438](../patents/DE705438.md) | 1941 | Winterling, Carl | The common guide plate ends at the rest position and individual fixed radial guide rails continue below the bars, with a small rider pressed into each bar's slot sliding on the rail. |

| [DE707259](../patents/DE707259.md) | 1941 |  | Slide-type pressure members and the type bars coupled to them are pulled away from the platen with a jerk right after printing, by cam links on the main shaft. |

| [DE709118](../patents/DE709118.md) | 1941 |  | A slide behind the front plate moves transversely to the springs and moves both ends of the retaining plate equally, with a slot in the front panel as indicator. |
| [DE711161](../patents/DE711161.md) | 1941 | Going, George Gould | Return spring anchors move on an arc so the spring tension changes mainly near the print point, leaving the start of the stroke light. |
| [DE714100](../patents/DE714100.md) | 1941 |  | Rests for the type levers and key levers are made of sound-damping material about half a type lever long, with a slot to absorb rebound, and the linkage is aligned so it cannot reverse at its joints. |
| [DE765018](../patents/DE765018.md) | 1942 |  | Tilt the whole type engine, carriers, casing, carriage support and switching parts as one unit forward and downward by an acute angle about the platen axis, so the line is visible while the types still strike radially. |
| [DE718687](../patents/DE718687.md) | 1942 | Broenner, Josef | Intermediate lever slideways are set at varying angles from the centre outward, so the drive acceleration increases toward the outer levers. |
| [DE718676](../patents/DE718676.md) | 1942 |  | Power-driven intermediate levers, moved by the typewriter's type levers through a transmission, select the punch functions by key, giving a constant key stroke. |
| [DE720162](../patents/DE720162.md) | 1942 |  | A package of leaf springs of decreasing length, clamped at one end, engage the universal rail one after another so the return force builds gradually. |
| [DE720869](../patents/DE720869.md) | 1942 |  | A carriage-dependent locking member moves into the path of the type lever carrying the zero, blocking that type when needed. |
| [DE723418](../patents/DE723418.md) | 1942 | Stingl, Ernst | Angle levers in each key row sit on a common straight axis and tie rods attach directly to the type levers, with all articulation points in one straight line so every key gets the same power transmission. |
| [DE724444](../patents/DE724444.md) | 1942 | Theo, Woehrle | Parallel pliers with jaws kinked twice in the same sense grip the type lever at its end and have notches to clear the stiffening rib. |
| [DE725865](../patents/DE725865.md) | 1942 | Gundlfinger, Karl | Horizontal feeler levers and vertical selector flaps pivot draw hooks into the pressure rail; the hooks are on double-armed links under the type levers. |

| [DE727098](../patents/DE727098.md) | 1942 | Maul, Michael | Printed numerals are shaped with line lengths graded evenly by value so that a photocell can tell them apart. |
| [DE730121](../patents/DE730121.md) | 1943 | Winter, Otto | The buffer ring sits on a vulcanised rubber liner held in a mounting ring fixed to the type lever, so it has no metal contact with the segment. |
| [DE730792](../patents/DE730792.md) | 1943 | Baer, Karl Heinz | Keep vertical key rods that drive horizontal typebars through a large and a small gear with racks. |
| [DE734728](../patents/DE734728.md) | 1943 | Loewe, Richard | A detachable, collapsible circular book typewriter: a loose circular writing device with keys in concentric circles sits on a foldable rail frame that carries the paper and does line feed. |
| [DE735479](../patents/DE735479.md) | 1943 | Vogt, Alfred Richard Georg | A new type lever drive replaces the slot-and-nipple connection so that the drive keeps working through the strike and the lever does not lose leverage near the platen. |

| [DE738926](../patents/DE738926.md) | 1943 | Grobe, Otto | A stop body of thin spring steel with an enclosed air space is set in a dovetail groove of the type lever bearing, with the travel limited to a fraction of a millimetre, so the type lever stops at a precise position without much noise. |

| [DE891697](../patents/DE891697.md) | 1949 | Kuentzel, Hellmuth | A tubular bearing piece with hollow conical ends sits on conical pins in two cheeks held under spring tension, so wear is taken up elastically and lubricant stays inside. |
